Lettuce and PV model

A simple hour-by-hour view of the modeled agrivoltaic system · 2026-01-01 to 2026-12-31

Generated from commit ec4b24853cb9be22b85a0f80052a4d9d46b8f120; working tree dirty: true.

This report is generated directly from the Python model and is independent of the existing dashboard. GHI, DNI and DHI come directly from the Period Components export. Crop light is calculated by the agrivoltaic radiation model. Values are hourly averages unless shown as daily totals. Daily DLI and PV energy use trapezoidal integration over finite adjacent samples and are shown only when at least 80% of expected daylight source samples are present. The source does not document the sensor type, units, or physical locations of its grid channels; channel comparisons are therefore exploratory and assumption-based, not confirmed bed-level validation. Temperature-dependent calculations use the supplemental typical-year air-temperature series (TMY climatological air temperature from tmy_air_temperature.csv, UTC month/day/hour mapped to local standard-time hours; first value 23.8°C). It is not measured temperature from the irradiance year, so biomass outputs are uncalibrated scenario results, not field predictions. The continuous biomass state is not a per-harvest yield. The Period Components export records daytime samples; unrecorded irradiance timestamps are assumed to have zero light in the model. This assumption does not alter measured source values or the reported daylight-coverage checks. Crop-cycle fresh-mass equivalents are separate from the continuous dry-biomass state and can be higher in cool seasons because the GDD clock makes those cycles longer; they are not calibrated or observed harvests.

Exploratory channel comparison

Open scientific task: obtain the authoritative site channel map, including sensor type, units, calibration, and physical placement. These values are exploratory, not independent validation: only for comparison, each channel is assumed to be shortwave irradiance in W/m² and converted with PAR fraction 0.45 and 4.57 µmol/J. A different sensor type could materially change the comparison. Do not use these metrics to calibrate crop-light conversion until the channel map is verified. Channels are shown by export name; no bed mapping is asserted. DLI errors are omitted because daylight sampling coverage is incomplete.
